\section{What is tabulated}

A degenerate vibrational mode $\Gamma_s$ of dimension $d$ has creation operators $a^\dagger_1,\ldots,a^\dagger_d$, and its $v$-quantum states span $\operatorname{Sym}^{v}\Gamma_s$. The point group acts by Lemma~2 of the paper, $U(\gel)\,a^\dagger_iU(\gel)^\dagger=\sum_jD_{j,i}(\gel)\,a^\dagger_j$, and the symmetry-adapted states (SALCs) $\cgket{\Gamma}{\mu}{\mult}$ are obtained with the matrix-valued projectors $P^\Gamma_{\mu,\nu}=(d_\Gamma/|\grp{G}|)\sum_{\gel\in\grp{G}}D^\Gamma_{\mu,\nu}(\gel)^*U(\gel)$ (Theorem~1): copies $\mult$ by projecting occupation states in order, partners by $P^\Gamma_{\mu,1}$. This is the construction of \texttt{salc\_fock\_basis.wl}. The groups are those of \texttt{species\_tables/pointgroups.py}, and the labels are o3harmonics' (\texttt{species\_tables/o3h\_tex.py}). The irrep matrices are pointgroups' own, not the o3harmonics matrices that the species tables import, so the vibrational carriers here are in the bases of the rotational and nuclear-spin carriers of the species tables except for the two-dimensional irreps of the ket map below and the $\irr{g}$, $\irr{h}$ of $\grp{I}$ and $\grp{I_{h}}$, whose bases are given in their group sections.

\emph{Kets.} A two-dimensional mode is written $\ket{n_+,n_-}$, with $n_-$ quanta in partner~1 of its basis and $n_+$ in partner~2, and vibrational angular momentum $\ell=n_+-n_-$. o3harmonics' harmonic degree is $|\ell|$, and the sign of $\ell$ picks the partner within its harmonic layer $H_{|\ell|}$ ($\ell<0$: partner~1 of the basis here). The basis is the one in which $\elt{C_{\mathnormal{n}}}$ is diagonal, $\diag(\expe^{\iunit\theta},\expe^{-\iunit\theta})$, and a swap acts as the plain permutation, so $U(\elt{C_{\mathnormal{n}}})\ket{n_+,n_-}=\expe^{-\iunit\theta\ell}\ket{n_+,n_-}$, that is $\expe^{-2\pi\iunit q\ell/n}$ on a mode on which $\elt{C_{\mathnormal{n}}}$ turns by $\theta=2\pi q/n$. In o3harmonics' partners the ket is $\occ{n_-n_+}$; it is $(-1)^{n_+}\occ{n_-n_+}$ for the 18 irreps whose partner~2 has the opposite sign there, and $(-1)^{n_-}\occ{n_+n_-}$ for $\irr{e}_{3}$ of $\grp{D_{4d}}$; $\irr{e}_{4}$, $\irr{e}_{5}$ of $\grp{D_{6d}}$; and $\irr{e}_{5}$, $\irr{e}_{6}$, $\irr{e}_{7}$ of $\grp{D_{8d}}$ (\texttt{species\_tables/o3h\_print.json}). A complex pair $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_k\oplus\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_k$ is a mode whose first member has character $\expe^{+2\pi\iunit k/n}$ on the generator; o3harmonics labels that member $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_k$. Three-dimensional modes are written $\occ{n_xn_yn_z}$, in the partners $x$, $y$, $z$ of o3harmonics. Coefficients are exact where they are recognised ($\rootunity{n}=\expe^{2\pi\iunit/n}$); the remaining icosahedral ones are given to four decimals.

\emph{Phases.} In a residue table the first partner of a SALC has a positive coefficient on $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, or on $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ when that is its only ket; in a Law-1 table every partner is one ket with coefficient~1 or $(\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}\pm\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell})/\sqrt2$. A one-dimensional SALC of a single mode can therefore appear with opposite overall signs in the two tables, as $\irr{a}_{2}$ of $\grp{C_{3v}}$ does; both are SALCs.

\section{Three classifications}

A table for every $v$ is unnecessary, because the SALCs of all $v$ follow from a finite amount of data. Three facts reduce it.

\begin{proposition}[Parity]
The vibrational angular momentum $\ell$ of a two-dimensional mode, and the harmonic degree $\ell$ of a vector-like three-dimensional mode, have the parity of $v$. The inversion $\elt{i}$ or the reflection $\elt{\sigma_{h}}$ acts on $v$ quanta of a $\ptag{u}$ or $''$ mode as $(-1)^v$; hence the tags $\ptag{g}$/$\ptag{u}$ and $'$/$''$ alternate with $v$ for such modes and are constant for $\ptag{g}$ and $'$ modes.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Residues, $d=2$]
In these bases every group element acts on a two-dimensional mode as $s\diag(\expe^{\iunit\theta},\expe^{-\iunit\theta})$ or $s$ times a swap, with $s=\pm1$. Hence it maps $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ to a multiple of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ or $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, every SALC lies in $\mathrm{span}\{\ket{n_+,n_-},\ket{n_-,n_+}\}$, and its species and coefficients depend only on $\ell$ modulo $a_2$, the least common multiple of the orders of the nonzero matrix entries of the mode: the phase of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ under an element is such an entry to the power $-\ell$, because $v-\ell$ is even. One table with $a_2$ rows therefore gives the SALCs for every $v$. For an irreducible mode this $a_2$ is o3harmonics' second exponent of the denominator $(1-t^{a_1})(1-t^{a_2})$ of the mode's generating function.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Harmonics, vector-like $d=3$]
If $D(\gel)=\lambda(\gel)\,\gmat$ for a one-dimensional irrep $\lambda$ (the mode is then o3harmonics' defining irrep, or its twist by $\lambda$), the invariant $r^2=a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger=\sum_ia^{\dagger2}_i$ and its adjoint commute with $U(\gel)$, and
\[
\operatorname{Sym}^{v}\Gamma_s=\bigoplus_{\ell=v,v-2,\ldots}r^{v-\ell}H_\ell,\qquad H_\ell=\ker(a\cdot a)\cap\operatorname{Sym}^{\ell}\Gamma_s,\quad\dim H_\ell=2\ell+1 .
\]
Every SALC of $v$ quanta is $r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell/\lVert r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell\rVert$, with $\psi_\ell$ a $\grp{G}$-adapted harmonic SALC and $\lVert r^{2k}\psi_\ell\rVert^2=4^kk!\,(\ell+\tfrac32)_k$, $k=(v-\ell)/2$. The species of $H_\ell$ are the rotational branching $\restr{\irrJp{\ell}{(-1)^\ell}}{\grp{G}}$, of $\grp{O}(3)$ parity $(-1)^\ell$, times $\lambda^\ell$: the same multiplicities as the rotational sector of the paper, and the copy label of a repeated irrep becomes the layer $\ell$.
\end{proposition}

The first two are elementary; the third is the isotropic-oscillator decomposition restricted to $\grp{G}$. Modes covered by neither (the $\irr{t}_{2}$, $\irr{g}$, $\irr{h}$ modes of $\grp{I}$ and $\grp{I_{h}}$) are listed explicitly, the $\irr{t}_{2}$ modes for $v\le6$ and the $\irr{g}$ and $\irr{h}$ modes for $v\le3$ (their $v=6$ manifolds have 84 and 210 states with irrational coefficients), together with their multiplicities to $v=12$.

\emph{Checks.} \texttt{salc\_tables.py} stops unless all of the following hold. Each residue table is compared with the projector construction for every $v\le2a_2+2$ and every $\ell$, and the resulting states are verified to be orthonormal, complete, and covariant. Each harmonic table is verified the same way for $\ell\le6$, the norm formula and the factorised SALCs are verified for $v\le6$, and the harmonic species are computed from $\operatorname{Sym}^{\ell}\Gamma_s-\operatorname{Sym}^{\ell-2}\Gamma_s$. Every explicit table is verified for orthonormality, completeness, and covariance. The multiplicities agree with GTPack for 32 crystallographic groups. In all, the tables cover 80 two-dimensional modes, 67 separably degenerate pairs, 14 vector-like three-dimensional modes, and 9 further modes.

\section{Combination bands}

Quanta in several distinct modes form combination bands. The overtone tables determine them through four laws.

\begin{proposition}[Law 0: product]
For distinct modes $s=1,2,\ldots$ the manifold is the ordinary product $\mathcal H(v_1,v_2,\ldots)=\bigotimes_s\operatorname{Sym}^{v_s}\Gamma_s$; only quanta in the same mode are symmetrised, so two different modes of the same species are distinct factors. The multiplicities have the generating function
\[
\sum_{v_1,v_2,\ldots}m_\Gamma(v_1,v_2,\ldots)\,t_1^{v_1}t_2^{v_2}\cdots=\frac1{|\grp{G}|}\sum_{\gel\in\grp{G}}\frac{\chi_\Gamma(\gel)^*}{\prod_s\det\bigl(1-t_sD^{\Gamma_s}(\gel)\bigr)} .
\]
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Law 1: total vibrational angular momentum, $d=2$]
Let the two-dimensional modes of a group be acted on diagonally by the subgroup $\grp{H}$ (index $1$ or $2$), generated by a principal element $\pel$ of order $N$ and, where needed, a parity element $\qel$, with $D^{\Gamma_s}(\pel)=\diag(\expe^{2\pi\iunit k_s/N},\expe^{-2\pi\iunit k_s/N})$ and $D^{\Gamma_s}(\qel)=\diag\bigl((-1)^{q_s},(-1)^{q_s}\bigr)$. The product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n^{(s)}_+,n^{(s)}_-}$ transforms under $\grp{H}$ with the character fixed by
\[
\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod N,\qquad p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod 2 ,
\]
and the plain swap maps it to $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$ (all $\ell_s\to-\ell_s$). Hence every combination SALC lies in $\mathrm{span}\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$, and its species and coefficients depend only on $(\ell_{\mathrm{tot}},p)$: a two-dimensional irrep with partners $\ket{\pm\boldsymbol\ell}$ if the $\grp{H}$-character is complex, and two one-dimensional irreps $(\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}\pm\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell})/\sqrt2$ if it is real. Each group section lists $k_s$, $q_s$, and the table $(\ell_{\mathrm{tot}},p)\mapsto$ species, which covers every combination band of its two-dimensional modes, for any number of modes and quanta. For one mode it reduces to the residue tables.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Law 2: coupling of harmonic layers, vector-like $d=3$]
For vector-like modes $D_s(\gel)=\lambda_s(\gel)\,\gmat$,
\[
\operatorname{Sym}^{a}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{b}\Gamma_2=\bigoplus_{\ell_1,\ell_2}\ \bigoplus_{\ell=|\ell_1-\ell_2|}^{\ell_1+\ell_2}\restr{\irrJp{\ell}{+}}{\grp{G}}\otimes\bigl[\det\nolimits_{\grp{G}}^{\ell_1+\ell_2}\lambda_1^{\ell_1}\lambda_2^{\ell_2}\bigr],
\]
with $\ell_1=a,a-2,\ldots$ and $\ell_2=b,b-2,\ldots$: the harmonic layers of the two modes couple like ordinary angular momenta and the result is restricted to $\grp{G}$. The parity is that of $\ell_1+\ell_2$, not of $\ell$. The explicit states are $r_1^{a-\ell_1}r_2^{b-\ell_2}$, with $r_s^2=a^\dagger_s\!\cdot a^\dagger_s$ the invariant of mode $s$, times $\grp{SO}(3)$ Clebsch--Gordan combinations of the harmonic SALCs, projected onto $\grp{G}$.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Law 3: general]
For any other pair (a two- with a three-dimensional mode, or the $\irr{g}$, $\irr{h}$ modes of $\grp{I}$, $\grp{I_{h}}$) the species follow from the product table of $\grp{G}$ applied to the single-mode decompositions, and the SALCs from the point-group Clebsch--Gordan coefficients or, equivalently, from the projectors on the product space. Cubic $\irr{e}$ modes factor through $\Sym{3}$, so their part of a mixed band still follows Law~1.
\end{proposition}

\emph{Checks.} Law~1 is verified in every group with two-dimensional modes, for every pair of modes (including two distinct modes of one species) and $1\le v_1,v_2\le4$, and for every triple of modes at $(v_1,v_2,v_3)=(1,1,1),(2,1,1),(1,2,3)$: the states it prescribes are orthonormal, complete, and covariant (8491 product spaces). Law~2 is verified for every pair of vector-like modes and $1\le a,b\le4$ against the product characters (384 cases). The worked combination bands of Sec.~\ref{sec:combex}, among them $\grp{C_{3v}}$ $\operatorname{Sym}^{6}\irr{e}\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{3}\irr{e}=5\,\irr{a}_{1}\oplus5\,\irr{a}_{2}\oplus9\,\irr{e}$, are produced and verified by the projector construction.


\section{Worked examples at finite $v$}\label{sec:examples}

Explicit SALCs of one mode for $v\le6$, from the projector construction; each set is orthonormal, complete, and transforms with the irrep matrices. They are special cases of the tables of Sec.~\ref{sec:groups}.

\section{Worked combination bands}\label{sec:combex}

Explicit SALCs of combination bands, from the projector construction on the product of the single-mode Fock spaces (Law~0); each set is orthonormal, complete, and covariant. Kets are products, first mode first. Two equal labels denote two distinct modes of the same species (e.g.\ $\nu_3$ and $\nu_4$).

\clearpage\section{All point groups}\label{sec:groups}

For every group of the species tables: residue tables for two-dimensional modes (for $\ell>0$ each row gives the SALC on the pair $\ket{n_+,n_-}$, $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, partners separated by commas), single-row residue lists for separably degenerate pairs, harmonic tables for vector-like three-dimensional modes, and explicit tables otherwise. Linear molecules: for $\grp{C_{\infty v}}$ and $\grp{D_{\infty h}}$ the residue rule holds with $a_2\to\infty$, the pair $\ell=\pm\Xi$ forming $\irr{e}_{\Xi}$ ($\Pi,\Delta,\Phi,\ldots$ for $\Xi=1,2,3,\ldots$) with partners $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, $\ket{n_+,n_-}$, and $\ell=0$ giving $\irr{a}_{1}$ of $\grp{C_{\infty v}}$ and $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}$ of $\grp{D_{\infty h}}$; for $\grp{D_{\infty h}}$ the parity is $(-1)^v$ for an $\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}}$ mode.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{1}}$ \quad (order 1)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{1}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{1h}}$ \quad (order 2)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{1h}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{S_{2}}$ \quad (order 2)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{S_{2}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{2}}$ \quad (order 2)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{2}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{3}}$ \quad (order 3)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{3}}$}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 3$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}$\\
1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$\\
2 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$): $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e})=1$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{4}}$ \quad (order 4)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{4}}$}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 4$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 4$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}$\\
1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$\\
2 & $\irr{b}$\\
3 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{4}}$ ($N=4$): $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e})=1$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 4$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\irr{b}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{5}}$ \quad (order 5)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{5}}$}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1}$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 5$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 5$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}$\\
1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1}$\\

3 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1}$\\
4 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2}$\\

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{2v}}$ \quad (order 4)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{2v}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{3v}}$ \quad (order 6)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{3v}}$}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}$ ($a_2=3$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:
\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
1 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$): $k(\irr{e})=1$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$.

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{4v}}$}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}$ ($a_2=4$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:

 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\

2 & $\irr{b}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{b}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
3 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

3 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
4 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
5 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
6 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}_{2}$ ($a_2=7$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:
\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell\bmod 7$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
1 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
3 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
4 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
5 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
6 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}_{3}$ ($a_2=7$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:
\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell\bmod 7$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
1 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
3 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
4 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
5 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
6 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{7}}$ ($N=7$): $k(\irr{e}_{1})=1$, $k(\irr{e}_{2})=2$, $k(\irr{e}_{3})=3$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 7$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & $\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & $\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

6 & $\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{2h}}$ \quad (order 4)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{2h}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{C_{3h}}$ \quad (order 6)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{C_{3h}}$}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}'\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}'$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 3$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}'$\\
1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}'$\\
2 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}'$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}''\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}''$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 6$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 6$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}'$\\
1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}''$\\
2 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}'$\\
3 & $\irr{a}''$\\
4 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}'$\\
5 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}''$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{S_{3}}$ ($N=6$): $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}'\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}')=2$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}''\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}'')=5$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 6$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}'$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}''$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}'$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}'$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}''$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{4}}$ ($N=4$), $\qel=\elt{\sigma_{h}}$: $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=1$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=1$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=1$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 4$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

0 & 1 & $\irr{a}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\irr{b}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 1 & $\irr{b}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 0 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 1 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{6}}$ ($N=6$), $\qel=\elt{\sigma_{h}}$: $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}})=1$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}})=1$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}})=2$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}})=0$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}})=1$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}})=0$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}})=2$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}})=1$. \paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{S_{16}}$ ($N=16$): $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1})=1$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2})=2$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{3}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{3})=3$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{4}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{4})=4$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{5}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{5})=5$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{6}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{6})=6$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{7}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{7})=7$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 16$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{4}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

9 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{7}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

10 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{6}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

11 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{5}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

12 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{4}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

13 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{3}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

14 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{2}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

15 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{1}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$\grp{D_{2}}$ \quad (order 4)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{D_{2}}$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\occ{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\subsection*{$\grp{D_{3}}$ \quad (order 6)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{D_{3}}$}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}$ ($a_2=3$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:
\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
1 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$): $k(\irr{e})=1$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$.

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{D_{4}}$}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}$ ($a_2=4$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:

 & $\irr{b}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{8}}$ ($N=8$), $\qel=\elt{\sigma_{h}}$: $k(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}})=1$, $q(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}})=1$, $k(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}})=2$, $q(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}})=0$, $k(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{g}})=3$, $q(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{g}})=1$, $k(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}})=1$, $q(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}})=0$, $k(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}})=2$, $q(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}})=1$, $k(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{u}})=3$, $q(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{u}})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 8$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

0 & 1 & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{3\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{3\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & 0 & $\irr{b}_{1\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{b}_{2\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & 1 & $\irr{b}_{1\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{b}_{2\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{3\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{3\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

6 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

6 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

7 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

7 & 1 & $\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$, $p=0$.

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{S_{14}}$ ($N=14$): $k(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{g}})=8$, $k(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{g}})=2$, $k(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{g}})=10$, $k(\irr{e}_{1\ptag{u}})=1$, $k(\irr{e}_{2\ptag{u}})=9$, $k(\irr{e}_{3\ptag{u}})=3$. If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$.

\subsection*{$\grp{T}$ \quad (order 12)}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 3$ (all integers $\ell$):

1 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$\\
2 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $\irr{t}$, vector-like: $D(\gel)=\lambda(\gel)\,\gmat$ with $\lambda=\irr{a}$.} Every SALC is $r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell/\lVert r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell\rVert$ with $\psi_\ell$ a harmonic SALC below, $r^2=a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger$ and $\lVert r^{2k}\psi_\ell\rVert^2=4^kk!\,(\ell+\tfrac32)_k$, $k=(v-\ell)/2$. \paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$), $\qel=\elt{C_{2}}$: $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e})=1$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

Multiplicities of the combination bands $\operatorname{Sym}^{v_1}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{v_2}\Gamma_2$ of two distinct modes (Law~0; for vector-like modes they follow Law~2):

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{S_{6}^{5}}$ ($N=6$), $\qel=\elt{C_{2}}$: $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=2$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=0$, $k(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=5$, $q(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 6$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Every product state is a SALC, of species:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & 0 & $\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & 0 & $\conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

Multiplicities of the combination bands $\operatorname{Sym}^{v_1}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{v_2}\Gamma_2$ of two distinct modes (Law~0; for vector-like modes they follow Law~2):

\begin{longtable}{p{0.27\textwidth} c p{0.56\textwidth}}\hline modes & $(v_1,v_2)$ & decomposition\\ \hline\endhead

$(\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})\otimes (\conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})$ & $(1,1)$ & $2\,\irr{a}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{1}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\oplus \conjpair{2}\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$\\

\subsection*{$\grp{T_{d}}$ \quad (order 24)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{T_{d}}$}

\emph{Mode $\irr{e}$ ($a_2=3$).} $\ket{n_{\!+}\!=\!n_{\!-}}$ ($\ell=0$, even $v$): $\irr{a}_{1}$. For $\ell>0$, with $n_\pm=(v\pm\ell)/2$:
\begin{longtable}{c l l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
 & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_+,n_-}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
1 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_-,n_+}$,\quad $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{n_+,n_-}$,\quad $\ket{n_-,n_+}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $\irr{t}_{1}$, vector-like: $D(\gel)=\lambda(\gel)\,\gmat$ with $\lambda=\irr{a}_{2}$.} Every SALC is $r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell/\lVert r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell\rVert$ with $\psi_\ell$ a harmonic SALC below, $r^2=a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger$ and $\lVert r^{2k}\psi_\ell\rVert^2=4^kk!\,(\ell+\tfrac32)_k$, $k=(v-\ell)/2$. Species of the harmonic layer $H_\ell$:
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell$ & $H_\ell$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$\\
1 & $\irr{t}_{1}$\\
2 & $\irr{e}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}$\\
3 & $\irr{a}_{2}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}$\\
4 & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{e}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}$\\
5 & $\irr{e}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}$\\
6 & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{a}_{2}\oplus \irr{e}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
7 & $\irr{a}_{2}\oplus \irr{e}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
8 & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
9 & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{a}_{2}\oplus \irr{e}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
10 & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{a}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}\oplus 2\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
11 & $\irr{a}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
12 & $2\,\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{a}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus 3\,\irr{t}_{2}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$), $\qel=\elt{C_{2}}$: $k(\irr{e})=1$, $q(\irr{e})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$, $p=0$.

Multiplicities of the combination bands $\operatorname{Sym}^{v_1}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{v_2}\Gamma_2$ of two distinct modes (Law~0; for vector-like modes they follow Law~2):

 & $(1,2)$ & $\irr{a}_{1}\oplus \irr{a}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}$\\

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{C_{3}}$ ($N=3$), $\qel=\elt{C_{2}}$: $k(\irr{e})=1$, $q(\irr{e})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 3$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{1}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\irr{e}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$, $p=0$.

Multiplicities of the combination bands $\operatorname{Sym}^{v_1}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{v_2}\Gamma_2$ of two distinct modes (Law~0; for vector-like modes they follow Law~2):

\paragraph{Combination bands.}

Law~1 with $\pel=\elt{S_{6}^{5}}$ ($N=6$), $\qel=\elt{C_{2}}$: $k(\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=2$, $q(\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}})=0$, $k(\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=5$, $q(\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}})=0$. A product state $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}=\bigotimes_s\ket{n_+^{(s)},n_-^{(s)}}$ has $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=\sum_sk_s\ell_s\bmod 6$ and parity $p=\sum_sq_s\ell_s\bmod2$.

Its species, and the SALCs on $\{\ket{\boldsymbol\ell},\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}\}$ ($\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$: all $n_\pm$ exchanged), are:

\begin{longtable}{c c l l}\hline $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}$ & $p$ & species & SALC (partners)\\ \hline\endhead

0 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2\ptag{g}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

1 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

2 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

3 & 0 & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}+\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

& & $\irr{a}_{2\ptag{u}}$ & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

4 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

5 & 0 & $\irr{e}_{\ptag{u}}$ & $\ket{-\boldsymbol\ell}$,\quad $\ket{\boldsymbol\ell}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

If all $\ell_s=0$ the state is single and has the symmetric species of the row $\ell_{\mathrm{tot}}=0$, $p=0$.

Multiplicities of the combination bands $\operatorname{Sym}^{v_1}\Gamma_1\otimes\operatorname{Sym}^{v_2}\Gamma_2$ of two distinct modes (Law~0; for vector-like modes they follow Law~2):

\begin{longtable}{p{0.27\textwidth} c p{0.56\textwidth}}\hline modes & $(v_1,v_2)$ & decomposition\\ \hline\endhead

$\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}\otimes \irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$ & $(1,1)$ & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}\oplus \irr{a}_{2\ptag{g}}\oplus \irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$\\

 & $(1,2)$ & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}\oplus \irr{a}_{2\ptag{g}}\oplus 2\,\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$\\

 & $(1,3)$ & $\irr{a}_{1\ptag{g}}\oplus \irr{a}_{2\ptag{g}}\oplus 3\,\irr{e}_{\ptag{g}}$\\

\subsection*{$\grp{I}$ \quad (order 60)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{I}$}

Basis of the four- and five-dimensional irreps: the creation operators $a^\dagger_\mu$ of the mode transform like the following states of a vector-like mode ($\kett{n_xn_yn_z}$), with the $\ptag{u}$ irreps differing by the parity character:

\begin{longtable}{l c p{0.7\textwidth}}\hline irrep & $\mu$ & transforms like\\ \hline\endhead

$\irr{h}$ & 1 & $\sqrt{\tfrac{2}{3}}\kett{200}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{6}}\kett{020}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{6}}\kett{002}$\\

$\irr{h}$ & 2 & $\kett{110}$\\

$\irr{h}$ & 3 & $\kett{101}$\\

$\irr{h}$ & 4 & $\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\kett{020}-\sqrt{\tfrac{1}{2}}\kett{002}$\\

$\irr{h}$ & 5 & $\kett{011}$\\

$\irr{g}$ & 1 & $\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{10}}\kett{300}-(\sqrt{\frac{7}{20} - \frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20}})\kett{120}-(\sqrt{\frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20} + \frac{7}{20}})\kett{102}$\\

$\irr{g}$ & 2 & $(\sqrt{\frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20} + \frac{7}{20}})\kett{210}-\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{10}}\kett{030}+(\sqrt{\frac{7}{20} - \frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20}})\kett{012}$\\

$\irr{g}$ & 3 & $(\sqrt{\frac{7}{20} - \frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20}})\kett{201}+(\sqrt{\frac{3 \sqrt{5}}{20} + \frac{7}{20}})\kett{021}-\sqrt{\tfrac{3}{10}}\kett{003}$\\

$\irr{g}$ & 4 & $\kett{111}$\\

\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $\irr{t}_{1}$, vector-like: $D(\gel)=\lambda(\gel)\,\gmat$ with $\lambda=\irr{a}$.} Every SALC is $r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell/\lVert r^{v-\ell}\psi_\ell\rVert$ with $\psi_\ell$ a harmonic SALC below, $r^2=a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger$ and $\lVert r^{2k}\psi_\ell\rVert^2=4^kk!\,(\ell+\tfrac32)_k$, $k=(v-\ell)/2$. Species of the harmonic layer $H_\ell$:
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell$ & $H_\ell$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $\irr{a}$\\
1 & $\irr{t}_{1}$\\
2 & $\irr{h}$\\
3 & $\irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{g}$\\
4 & $\irr{g}\oplus \irr{h}$\\
5 & $\irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{h}$\\
6 & $\irr{a}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{g}\oplus \irr{h}$\\
7 & $\irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{g}\oplus \irr{h}$\\
8 & $\irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{g}\oplus 2\,\irr{h}$\\
9 & $\irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{g}\oplus \irr{h}$\\
10 & $\irr{a}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{g}\oplus 2\,\irr{h}$\\
11 & $2\,\irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus \irr{g}\oplus 2\,\irr{h}$\\
12 & $\irr{a}\oplus \irr{t}_{1}\oplus \irr{t}_{2}\oplus 2\,\irr{g}\oplus 2\,\irr{h}$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$\grp{I_{h}}$ \quad (order 120)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$\grp{I_{h}}$}

Basis of the four- and five-dimensional irreps: the creation operators $a^\dagger_\mu$ of the mode transform like the following states of a vector-like mode ($\kett{n_xn_yn_z}$), with the $\ptag{u}$ irreps differing by the parity character:
